Let's design final algorithm:
- Global deadline = budget_s - 30.
- Load height_sequence_1 if available, normalize. If not, generate initial shapes.
- Best sequence and score global.
- Define functions:
  - normalize(a, sum_target=None) with default sum=√(2n) if None? For final maybe keep scale? Evaluation invariant, but to avoid sum<0.01, use target maybe √(2n). If target too large? If values >1000? With sum √n, max values O(√n) for n up to 16k ~126, below1000. Good. Use S=√(2n) ~64 for 2048, values endpoints maybe few, safe.
  - score(a): FFT full conv, ensure nonnegative, return max(b) if sum=S? Wait score invariant; if a not normalized, return 2n max/sum^2. We can compute directly for any a. For normalized S=√(2n), score=max b. Use general for safety: S=sum; if S<1e-12 inf; maxb; return 2n maxb/S^2.
  - convolve_fft(a): m=np.next_power_of_two(2len-1), F=np.fft.fft(a,m); b=np.fft.ifft(F*F).real[:2n-1]; b=np.maximum(b,0) (should be nonnegative but numerical). Return b.
  - Optionally exact np.convolve for small n to validate.
  - diversify.
- LP step:
  def lp_improve(a, max_seconds, seed?, n_lp maybe a length):
    S = sqrt(2*n)
    a = normalize(a,S)
    deadline...
    best = a, best_sc=score(a)
    M = score(best)
    count=0
    while time left and maybe M > target:
      Build A for a.
      Solve linprog c=-ones, A_ub=A, b_ub=M*1e-9? Actually constraints A g ≤ M*(1+1e-9). Add small epsilon.
      If status not 0, break or try smaller M? Since a feasible, should.
      g = x. If not finite or sum<=S*1.0001? If sum ≤ S*(1+1e-8), LP direction no movement; break.
      g = max(g,0) (LP may small negative).
      g_sum = sum(g)
      for t in [0.25,0.125,0.0625,0.5,0.1,0.05,0.03,0.01?]: h=(1-t)*a + t*(g*(S/g_sum)); h nonneg normalize S; sc=score; choose best.
      If best_t >0 and sc<best_sc -1e-10: update a,best, M; last_improve; count++; else break/converged.
      If no improvement, maybe try random perturbation and continue.
    return best,sc

def optimize_adam(a_init, n, max_seconds, seed, tau_start=0.5, tau_end=0.05):
    S=sqrt(2n); a=normalize(a_init,S); z=np.log(np.maximum(a, tiny)); z=z-z.mean(); m=np.next_pow2(2n-1)
    best=...; best_sc=score
    beta1=0.9,beta2=0.999,eps=1e-8
    mu=zeros,nu=zeros;t=0; last_improve=time
    dead=start+max_seconds
    while time<dead:
       p=softmax(z); # z clipped [-30,30]
       b=conv FFT with p*S? p*S.
       bmax=max(b)
       tau = tau_end + (tau_start-tau_end)*progress? plus stochastic.
       x=(b-bmax)/tau; x-=max; w=exp; w/=sum; maybe top boost? For max, weights on active high lags. We can use w = normalized; no top boost maybe.
       # exact gradient
       c = crosscorr(w,p) via FFT: w_rev = w[::-1] padded to m; fc=fft(p,m); fw=fft(w_rev,m); c=ifft(fc*fw).real[:n]
       gp = 2*c
       avg = dot(p,gp)
       gz = p*(gp-avg)
       # normalize
       grms=sqrt(mean(gz^2)); if small noise.
       # Adam update
       t+=1; mu=...; nu=...
       step = mhat/(sqrt(vhat)+eps)
       step = clip(step,-1,1)
       # line search: try z_new = z - lr*step for several lr factors
       lr = base*(1-progress)
       best_z=z; best_local=score(p) maybe expensive? use bmax.
       for factor in [1.0,0.5,0.25,0.1,0.5?]:
           zn=z - lr*factor*step
           center, clip
           pn=softmax(zn); sc=score exact (bmax); if sc<best_local: choose.
       z=best_z
       if best_local<best_sc: update best, last_improve
       if no improve for >60s: random kick
```

```
def weighted_gradient(a, w, m):
    # a 1D length n (e.g. p), w length 2n-1 normalized sum 1
    w_rev = np.zeros(m); w_rev[:w.size] = w[::-1]
    F = np.fft.fft(a, m)
    W = np.fft.fft(w_rev, m)
    c = np.fft.ifft(F * W).real[:n]
    return c
```

Implementation:
```
def gradient_from_weights(a, w, m):
    # a length n (p or p*S? use p for unit), w length L normalized
    w_rev = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
    w_rev[:w.size] = w[::-1]
    A = np.fft.fft(a, m)
    W = np.fft.fft(w_rev, m)
    conv = np.fft.ifft(A * W).real
    return conv[w.size-1 : w.size+n-2? Wait m index L-1-p]
```

def optimize_adam(a_init, n, max_seconds, seed):
    S=math.sqrt(2.0*n)
    a=_normalize(a_init,S); p=a/S
    z=np.log(np.maximum(p,1e-300)); z-=z.mean()
    m2=_next_pow2(2*n-1); mg=_next_pow2(3*n-2)
    best_p=p.copy(); best_sc=score_p(p); last=time
    beta1... 
    base_lr=0.12
    while time<deadline:
       p=softmax(z)
       b_p=conv_p(p,m2)
       bpmax=b_p.max()
       sc=2n*bpmax
       M=sc # equivalent
       # choose weights
       if rng.random()<0.5: hard active
          delta = M*(0.05 + 0.10*progress?) maybe in score units; for b_p threshold = M - delta/(2n)? Since b_p max=bpmax=M/(2n). Use bpmax. delta_b = bpmax*(0.02 + 0.1*(1-progress))
          active = b_p >= bpmax - delta_b
          if sum active<1 active=top1
          w = uniform active
       else soft tau
          tau = max(1e-6, bpmax*(0.03 + 0.12*(1-progress))) # b_p units
          x=(b_p-bpmax)/tau; x-=x.max(); w=exp; w/=sum
          # maybe top boost
       c=grad_cross(p,w,mg)
       gp=2*c
       avg=np.dot(p,gp)
       gz=p*(gp-avg)
       # remove mean? softmax invariant; gz sum0. Maybe center gz subtract mean.
       gz -= gz.mean()
       grms=np.linalg.norm(gz)/sqrt(n); if small, noise continue.
       # Adam
       step = ... normalize?
       step = mhat/(sqrt(vhat)+eps); step = step / max(1, norm)
       # line search factors
       lr=base_lr*(1 - 0.6*progress)
       best_z=z; best_val=sc
       for fct in (1.0,0.5,0.25):
           zn=z - lr*fct*step
           zn=zn- zn.mean(); zn=clip
           pn=softmax(zn); sc2=score_p(pn,m2)
           if sc2<best_val: best_z=zn; best_val=sc2
       z=best_z
       if best_val<best_sc: update best
       if no improve >60s: kick z += normal
    return normalize best_p*S, best_sc
```

def _next_pow2(x):
   x=int(max(1,x)); p=1; while p<x: p<<=1; return p
```

def _proj_nonneg(a):

def _normalize_sum(a, target):
   a=np.maximum(np.asarray(a,float).ravel(),0); s=a.sum(); if s<=0: a=np.ones_like(a); s=a.sum(); return a*target/s

def _make_inits(n, rng):
   res=[]
   t=linspace(0,1,n); i=arange(n)
   res.append(ones)
   # left/right power with exponent p
   for p in (0.3,0.5,0.6,0.7):

      res.append((n-i)**-p)
      res.append(((i+1.0)**-p + (n-i)**-p)*maybe)

```
def _lp_min_cross(a_S):
   n=len(a_S); S=math.sqrt(2.0*n)
   a=a_S.copy(); a_sum=a.sum(); a=(a*a_sum? no) ensure sum S: a=_normalize_sum(a,S)
   M=_score(a) # since sum S returns maxb
   # clip tiny
   f=np.maximum(a, 1e-12*max(1.0,a.max()))
   f=_normalize_sum(f,S)
   M=_score(f)
   L=2*n-1
   if n>6000: return None
   diags=[]; offsets=[]
   # include only >1e-15? If skipping too many, A maybe. Use all for safety.
   for d in range(n):
      val=f[d]
      if val <= 1e-15: continue
      diags.append(val*np.ones(n-d, dtype=float))
      offsets.append(d)
   if not diags: return None
   A=sparse.diags(diags, offsets=offsets, shape=(L,n), format='csr')
   # add t column
   t_col=sparse.csr_matrix((-1.0, (np.arange(L), np.zeros(L, dtype=int))), shape=(L,1))
   A_ub=sparse.hstack((A,t_col), format='csr')
   c=np.zeros(n+1); c[n]=1.0
   A_eq=np.zeros((1,n+1)); A_eq[0,:n]=1.0
   bounds=[(0.0, S)]*n + [(0.0, None)]
   opts={'presolve': True, 'dual_feasibility_tolerance':1e-8, 'primal_feasibility_tolerance':1e-8, 'simplex_iteration_limit': 2000000}
   try: opts['time_limit']=90.0
   except: pass
   res=linprog(c, A_ub=A_ub, b_ub=np.zeros(L), A_eq=A_eq, b_eq=np.array([S]), bounds=bounds, method='highs', options=opts)
   if res is None: return None
   g=res.x[:n]
   if not finite all: return None
   g=np.clip(g,0,S)
   gs=g.sum()
   if gs<=0: return None
   g*=S/gs
   # maybe compute t_actual = max(conv(f,g)) and return
   return g
```

`_conv_b(a, m)`:
```
def _convolve_self(a, m):
    n=a.size
    F=np.fft.fft(a,m)
    b=np.fft.ifft(F*F).real[:2*n-1]
    b=np.maximum(b,0.0)
    return b
```
If a sum1, b small. `b.max()` maybe negative? clip.

`_score(a)`:
```
a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel(); n=a.size
if n==0: inf
S=a.sum(); if not finite or S<=1e-15: return inf
b=_convolve_self(a, _next_pow2(2*n-1))
bmax=b.max()
if not finite or bmax<=0: return 0 if S? but if all zero invalid; return inf
return float(2.0*n*bmax/(S*S))
```
If a tiny sum, S^2 small; but final sum1. Good.

`_soft(z)`:
```
def _softmax(z):
   z=np.asarray(z,float).copy(); zmax=z.max(); if not finite: zfill0
   z=z-zmax; z=np.clip(z,-700,700); e=np.exp(z); s=e.sum(); if s<=0: return ones/n; return e/s
```

`_grad_cross(p,w,m)`:
```
def _grad_cross(p,w,m):
    n=p.size; L=w.size
    wr=np.zeros(m); wr[:w.size]=w[::-1]
    F=np.fft.fft(p,m)
    W=np.fft.fft(wr,m)
    conv=np.fft.ifft(F*W).real
    c=conv[L-n:L][::-1]
    return c
```
Check L-n = n-1, slice length n. For n=2 L=3,n-1=1, conv[1:3]=[c1,c0] reversed ->[c0,c1]? Wait expected c=[a1,a0]. conv[1]=a0, conv[2]=a1; reversed [a1,a0]. Good. For n=3 L=5,n-1=2, conv[2:5]=[a? for w0?] reversed c. Good.
Need m>=L+n-1=3n-2. We'll pass m_grad. If m smaller? Ensure `_grad_fft_len(n)` returns next_pow2(3*n-2). Use separate.
If m from score 2n-1 too small, wrap; so use mg.

def optimize_adam(a_init, n, max_seconds, seed):
    a=np.asarray(a_init,float).ravel()
    if a.size != n: a=_interp(a,n)
    a=_normalize_sum(a,1.0)
    sc=score(a)
    if not finite: a=ones/n; sc=2
    z=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300)); z-=z.mean(); z=clip(-25,25)
    best=a.copy(); best_sc=sc; best_z=z.copy()
    m2=_next_pow2(2*n-1); mg=_next_pow2(3*n-2)
    rng=default_rng(seed)
    beta1=0.9; beta2=0.9999; eps=1e-8
    mu=zeros; nu=zeros; t=0
    start=time; dead=min(max(dead? no, start+max_seconds), global?) We'll just start+max_seconds; caller ensures.
    last_imp=start; base_lr=0.15
    check_c=2 if n<2048 else 3
    while time < dead:
       progress=min(1,(time-start)/max_seconds)
       if time-last_imp > min(90, 0.3*max_seconds): # kick
           if best_sc finite:
              z=np.log(max(best,1e-300)); z-=mean; z+=rng.normal(0,0.15+n? ,n); z=clip
           else...
           last_imp=time; t=0; mu*=0.5; nu*=0.5
           continue
       p=softmax(z)
       b=_convolve_self(p,m2)
       bpmax=b.max(); sc=2n*bpmax
       # weights
       L=b.size; frac_active=0.002*(1-progress)+0.0005
       K=max(1,int(frac_active*L))
       if rng.random()<0.35:
           tau=max(bpmax*0.001, bpmax*(0.02+0.08*(1-progress)))
           x=(b-bpmax)/tau; x-=x.max(); x=clip(x,-50,50); w=exp; w/=sum
           # add top K? w[idx]+=0.3? previous boost. Let's add small boost to top K:
           if K>0: idx=argpartition(b,-K)[-K:]; w[idx]+=0.25; w/=w.sum()
       else:
           idx=argpartition(b,-K)[-K:]
           w=zeros(L); w[idx]=1.0/len(idx)
       c=_grad_cross(p,w,mg)
       gp=2*c
       avg=np.dot(p,gp)
       gz=p*(gp-avg)
       gz-=gz.mean()
       grms=np.sqrt(np.mean(gz*gz))
       if not finite or grms<1e-15:
           z+=rng.normal(0,0.05,n); z=center; continue
       # normalize gradient to RMS for Adam? Adam handles; but add step normalization
       t+=1
       mu=b1*mu+(1-b1)*gz
       nu=b2*nu+(1-b2)*(gz*gz)
       b1hat=1-b1**t; b2hat=1-b2**t
       mhat=mu/b1hat; vhat=nu/b2hat
       step=mhat/(np.sqrt(vhat)+eps)
       # bound step
       step=np.clip(step,-1,1)
       stprms=np.sqrt(np.mean(step*step))
       if stprms>0: step=step/stprms
       lr=base_lr*(1.0-0.65*progress)
       # line search 2 factors
       best_z=z; best_loc=sc
       for fct in (1.0,0.5):
           zn=z - lr*fct*step
           zn=zn-zn.mean(); zn=clip(-25,25)
           pn=softmax(zn)
           bn=_convolve_self(pn,m2); sc2=2n*bn.max()
           if finite and sc2<best_loc:
               best_loc=sc2; best_z=zn
       z=best_z
       if finite and best_loc < best_sc - 1e-12:
           best_sc=best_loc; best_z=z.copy(); best_p=softmax(z); last_imp=time
       # every check, if current best not updated but sc current maybe? already have best_loc
       # If no improve, maybe occasionally restore best
       if t % 100 == 0 and time-last_imp>20:
           # small pull back to best
           zb=np.log(max(best,1e-300)); z=0.7*z+0.3*zb; z=center
    p=softmax(z); sc=score(p)
    if sc<best_sc: best=p; best_sc=sc
    return best, best_sc
```

def _lp_stage(a1, deadline, max_n=2048):
    n=len(a1)
    if n > max_n: a1=_interp(a1,max_n); n=max_n; print
    aS=_normalize_sum(a1, sqrt(2n)); bestS=aS; best_sc=score(aS)
    ...
    while time < deadline - min_solve:
       f=np.maximum(bestS,1e-12); f=normalize
       M=score(f)
       g=_lp_min_cross(f)
       if g is None: break
       # line search
       best_h=bestS; best_sc_local=best_sc; best_t=0
       for t in (0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.3,0.5,0.75,1.0):
          h=(1-t)*bestS + t*g; h=max; h=normalize S
          sc=score(h)
          if sc<best_sc_local: ...
       if best_t>0 and best_sc_local<best_sc-1e-10:
          bestS=best_h; best_sc=best_sc_local; last=time; print
       else:
          # try one perturbation
          if no_imp<2: perturbed=bestS*(1+rng.normal(0,0.02)); normalize; h=...
          else break
    return normalize(bestS,1), best_sc

def _param_inits(n, rng):
   res=[]
   i=np.arange(n,dtype=float); one=np.ones(n); res.append(one/ n? normalize)
   for p in (0.3,0.45,0.5,0.55,0.6):
       res.append((i+1)**-p)
       res.append((n-i)**-p)
       res.append(((i+1)**-p + (n-i)**-p))
       res.append(((i+1)**-p + 0.5*one))
   res.append(1/sqrt((i+1)*(n-i+1)))
   for c in (0.2,0.5,1,2,4): res.append(exp(-c*i/n))
   # random Fourier
   for _ in range(4): a=1+...
   normalize and maybe diversity? Simple.

def _next_pow2(x):
    x=int(max(1,x)); p=1
    while p<x: p <<=1
    return p

def _as_nonneg_array(a):
    if a is None: return None
    arr=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    if arr.ndim!=1: arr=arr.ravel()
    if arr.size==0: return None
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(arr)): return None
    arr=np.maximum(arr,0.0)
    return arr

def _normalize_sum(arr, target):
    arr=_as_nonneg_array(arr)
    if arr is None: arr=np.ones(1); ...
    s=float(arr.sum())
    if s<=0.0 or not finite: arr=np.ones(arr.size); s=arr.size
    return arr*(target/s)

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    total_start=time.monotonic()
    budget=float(max(120,budget_s))
    deadline=total_start + budget - 25

    candidates=[]

    if arr is not None and arr.size>=2 and arr.sum()>0:
       best_a=arr/_sum; best_sc=_score(best_a)

    else:

    lp_fraction = 0.42
    lp_deadline = deadline - max(180, 0.55*budget?) We need leave time.
       remaining = deadline-total_start

           best_a,best_sc=_register(...)
    # Adam at n_lp if time

    # Larger lengths

    # If no larger but best_n<4096? if cur_n maybe 2048 includes 4096,8192

        a1, sc1 = optimize_adam(a0,L,alloc,seed_l)
        update
        # Maybe if score improved a lot, continue
    # final if best_n maybe low and time left? run Adam at current with remaining
    remaining=deadline-time
    if remaining > 90 and best_a.size > 0:
       a1,sc1=optimize_adam(best_a, len(best_a), remaining-20, seed)
       update
    # normalize final
    if best_a is None: best_a=np.ones(512)/512
    best_a=_normalize_sum(best_a,1.0)
    best_a=np.clip(best_a,0.0,1000.0)
    # exact validation optional

       if best_a.size <= 8192 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 3:
           exact=evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in best_a])
           print('fft choose score', best_sc, 'exact', exact)
           if not np.isfinite(exact):

    except Exception as e:
       print('exact skipped', e)
    return [float(x) for x in best_a]

def _run_linprog(...):
   for opts in ( {'presolve': True, 'simplex_iteration_limit': 2000000}, {'presolve': True}, {} ):
       try:
          return linprog(..., options=opts)
       except Exception:
          pass
   return None

But no nested functions? Could do loop inside. This is top-level helper `_lp_solve(aS)`? We can put in `_lp_min_cross`. But if linprog iterates matrix twice, memory? It may fail first due option, matrix built; second uses same. Fine.
However if failure due memory, retry wastes time. Use only safe opts? Let's use `{'presolve': True}`.

```python
"""
Search for nonnegative step-function heights minimizing
    2 * n * max(convolve(a, a)) / sum(a)^2.

This implementation is a hybrid:
  1. Reuses the supplied previous sequence when available.
  2. Uses an LP-based "best response" direction:
     for current f, solve
       min t
       s.t. f * g <= t, 1^T g = ||f||_1, g >= 0
     then line-search a convex update f <- (1-alpha) f + alpha g.
  3. Uses a corrected FFT subgradient/Adam optimizer on log-softmax
     heights for further polishing and for larger lengths.
  4. Progressively increases the length and keeps the best sequence found.

The code is self-contained, uses only allowed libraries, defines all
helpers at top level, and avoids lambdas/closures.
"""

import numpy as np
import math
import time

try:
    from scipy import sparse
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
except Exception:
    sparse = None
    linprog = None


def _next_pow2(x):
    x = int(max(1, x))
    p = 1
    while p < x:
        p <<= 1
    return p


def _as_nonneg_array(a):
    if a is None:
        return None
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if arr.ndim != 1:
        arr = arr.ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        return None
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(arr)):
        return None
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    return arr


def _normalize_sum(a, target):
    arr = _as_nonneg_array(a)
    if arr is None:
        arr = np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        arr = np.ones_like(arr, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(arr.size)
    return (float(target) / s) * arr


def _convolve_self(a, m):
    a = _as_nonneg_array(a)
    if a is None:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    L = 2 * n - 1
    F = np.fft.fft(a, m)
    b = np.fft.ifft(F * F).real[:L]
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    return b


def _score(a):
    arr = _as_nonneg_array(a)
    if arr is None:
        return float("inf")
    n = arr.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    S = float(arr.sum())
    if S <= 1e-15 or not np.isfinite(S):
        return float("inf")
    m = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    b = _convolve_self(arr, m)
    bmax = float(b.max()) if b.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(bmax) or bmax <= 0.0:
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * bmax / (S * S))


def _interp(a, n):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    arr = _as_nonneg_array(a)
    if arr is None or arr.size == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    if arr.size == n:
        return _normalize_sum(arr, 1.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, arr.size)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    y = np.interp(x_new, x_old, arr / s)
    y = np.maximum(y, 0.0)
    yy = float(y.sum())
    if yy <= 0.0:
        y = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        yy = float(y.sum())
    return y / yy


def _softmax(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if z.size == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    zmax = float(np.max(z))
    if not np.isfinite(zmax):
        z.fill(0.0)
        zmax = 0.0
    z = z - zmax
    z = np.clip(z, -700.0, 700.0)
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(z.shape, 1.0 / z.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return e / s


def _grad_cross_from_weights(p, w, m):
    # For p length n and weights w length 2n-1, returns c_i =
    # sum_j p_j * w_{i+j} (out-of-bounds weights are zero).
    p = _as_nonneg_array(p)
    w = _as_nonneg_array(w)
    if p is None or w is None:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    n = p.size
    L = w.size
    wr = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
    wr[:L] = w[::-1]
    Fp = np.fft.fft(p, m)
    Fw = np.fft.fft(wr, m)
    conv = np.fft.ifft(Fp * Fw).real
    return conv[L - n : L][::-1]


def _param_inits(n, rng):
    n = int(max(8, n))
    res = []
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    one = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)

    res.append(one.copy())

    for p in (0.25, 0.35, 0.45, 0.50, 0.55, 0.60, 0.65):
        left = np.maximum(i + 1.0, 1.0)
        right = np.maximum(n - i, 1.0)
        res.append(left ** (-p))
        res.append(right ** (-p))
        res.append(left ** (-p) + 0.35 * right ** (-p))
        res.append(0.35 * left ** (-p) + right ** (-p))
        res.append(left ** (-p) + 0.55 * one)
        res.append(right ** (-p) + 0.55 * one)

    res.append(1.0 / np.sqrt(np.maximum(i + 1.0, 1.0) * np.maximum(n - i, 1.0)))
    res.append(1.0 / np.sqrt(np.maximum(i + 1.0, 1.0)))
    res.append(1.0 / np.sqrt(np.maximum(n - i, 1.0)))

    for c in (0.2, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 4.0, 8.0):
        res.append(np.exp(-c * i / float(max(1, n - 1))))

    t = i / float(max(1, n - 1))
    res.append(1.0 + 0.45 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * t) + 0.25 * np.cos(4.0 * np.pi * t))
    res.append(1.0 + 0.45 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * t) + 0.25 * np.cos(4.0 * np.pi * t))

    kc = min(18, n)
    for _ in range(4):
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for k in range(1, kc + 1):
            amp = 0.30 / (float(k) ** 0.80)
            ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
            if rng.random() < 0.5:
                a += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * k * t + ph)
            else:
                a += amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * k * t + ph)
        res.append(a)

    out = []
    for a in res:
        a = _normalize_sum(a, 1.0)
        if a.size == n and np.isfinite(_score(a)):
            out.append(a)
    return out


def _register_candidate(best_a, best_sc, a, sc, candidates):
    if a is None:
        return best_a, best_sc
    sc = float(sc)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return best_a, best_sc
    arr = _as_nonneg_array(a)
    if arr is None or float(arr.sum()) <= 0.0:
        return best_a, best_sc
    arr = _normalize_sum(arr, 1.0)

    if best_a is None or sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
        best_a = arr.copy()
        best_sc = sc
        if len(candidates) < 8:
            candidates.append((sc, best_a.copy()))
    else:
        if len(candidates) < 8 and sc < best_sc + 0.02:
            candidates.append((sc, arr.copy()))

    return best_a, best_sc


def _lp_min_cross(aS, n_max=2048):
    if sparse is None or linprog is None:
        return None, None
    arr = _as_nonneg_array(aS)
    if arr is None:
        return None, None
    n = arr.size
    if n < 4 or n > n_max:
        return None, None

    S = math.sqrt(2.0 * float(n))
    a = _normalize_sum(arr, S)
    floor = 1e-12 * max(1.0, float(a.max()))
    f = np.maximum(a, floor)
    f = _normalize_sum(f, S)

    L = 2 * n - 1
    base_ones = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    diags = []
    offsets = []
    for d in range(n):
        val = float(f[d])
        if val > 1e-15:
            diags.append(val * base_ones)
            offsets.append(d)
    if not diags:
        return None, None

    try:
        A = sparse.diags(diags, offsets=offsets, shape=(L, n), format="csr")
        t_col = sparse.csr_matrix(
            (-1.0, (np.arange(L, dtype=np.int64), np.zeros(L, dtype=np.int64))),
            shape=(L, 1),
            dtype=np.float64,
        )
        A_ub = sparse.hstack((A, t_col), format="csr")
        b_ub = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        return None, None

    c = np.zeros(n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    c[n] = 1.0

    A_eq = np.zeros((1, n + 1), dtype=np.float64)
    A_eq[0, :n] = 1.0
    b_eq = np.array([S], dtype=np.float64)

    bounds = [(0.0, float(S))] * n + [(0.0, None)]

    try:
        opts = {"presolve": True}
        res = linprog(
            c,
            A_ub=A_ub,
            b_ub=b_ub,
            A_eq=A_eq,
            b_eq=b_eq,
            bounds=bounds,
            method="highs",
            options=opts,
        )
    except Exception:
        return None, None
    finally:
        try:
            del A
            del A_ub
        except Exception:
            pass

    if res is None or not getattr(res, "success", False):
        # Accept a returned solution only if it looks finite/feasible enough.
        if res is None or res.x is None:
            return None, None
        x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
        if x.size < n + 1:
            return None, None
    else:
        x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)

    if x.size < n + 1 or not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        return None, None

    g = np.clip(x[:n], 0.0, S)
    gs = float(g.sum())
    if gs <= 1e-15:
        g = _normalize_sum(a, S)
        gs = float(g.sum())
    if gs <= 1e-15:
        return None, None
    g = g * (S / gs)
    g = np.clip(g, 0.0, S)
    tval = float(x[n]) if x[n] >= 0.0 else float("nan")
    return g, tval


def _lp_update_once(aS):
    g, tval = _lp_min_cross(aS)
    if g is None:
        return aS, _score(aS), False

    n = aS.size
    S = math.sqrt(2.0 * float(n))
    orig = _score(aS)
    best_h = aS.copy()
    best_sc = orig
    best_alpha = 0.0

    for alpha in (0.04, 0.08, 0.16, 0.30, 0.50, 0.75, 1.0):
        h = (1.0 - alpha) * aS + alpha * g
        h = np.maximum(h, 0.0)
        h = _normalize_sum(h, S)
        sc = _score(h)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_h = h
            best_alpha = alpha

    improved = (best_alpha > 0.0) and (best_sc < orig - 1e-9)
    return best_h, best_sc, improved


def _lp_stage(a1, deadline, max_n=2048, rng=None):
    a1 = _normalize_sum(a1, 1.0)
    n = int(a1.size)
    if n == 0:
        return a1, _score(a1)
    if n > max_n:
        a1 = _interp(a1, max_n)
        n = max_n

    if n < 16 or sparse is None or linprog is None:
        return _normalize_sum(a1, 1.0), _score(a1)

    if rng is None:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(12345)

    S = math.sqrt(2.0 * float(n))
    aS = _normalize_sum(a1, S)
    bestS = aS.copy()
    best_sc = _score(aS)
    start = time.monotonic()

    if n <= 1024:
        min_before_solve = 25.0
    elif n <= 1536:
        min_before_solve = 60.0
    else:
        min_before_solve = 110.0

    no_improve = 0
    solves = 0
    print("LP stage start n=%d score=%.8f" % (n, best_sc))

    while True:
        remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if remaining <= min_before_solve:
            break
        if solves >= 60:
            break

        t0 = time.monotonic()
        h, sc, imp = _lp_update_once(bestS)
        dt = time.monotonic() - t0
        solves += 1

        if imp and np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-10:
            bestS = h
            best_sc = sc
            no_improve = 0
            print("  LP solve %d score=%.8f dt=%.1fs" % (solves, best_sc, dt))
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            print("  LP solve %d no improvement dt=%.1fs sc=%.8f" % (solves, dt, sc))
            if no_improve >= 2:
                break
            # Small perturbation to escape a local LP stagnation.
            eps = 0.005 + 0.01 * float(solves) / float(max(3, no_improve))
            pert = bestS * (1.0 + float(eps) * rng.normal(size=n))
            pert = np.maximum(pert, 0.0)
            if float(pert.sum()) > 0.0:
                bestS = _normalize_sum(pert, S)
            else:
                bestS = _normalize_sum(a1, S)

        # If HiGHS is unexpectedly slow, stop this LP stage safely.
        if dt > 5.0 * min_before_solve:
            print("  LP solve dt too large, stop LP stage")
            break

    return _normalize_sum(bestS, 1.0), best_sc


def _optimize_adam(a_init, n, max_seconds, seed):
    n = int(max(16, n))
    max_seconds = float(max(2.0, max_seconds))
    if isinstance(seed, (np.integer, int)):
        seed = int(seed)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    a = _interp(a_init, n)
    a = _normalize_sum(a, 1.0)
    best = a.copy()
    best_sc = _score(a)
    if not np.isfinite(best_sc):
        best = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        best_sc = 2.0
        a = best.copy()

    z = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
    z = z - float(z.mean())
    z = np.clip(z, -30.0, 30.0)
    best_z = z.copy()

    m_score = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    m_grad = _next_pow2(3 * n - 2)

    beta1 = 0.90
    beta2 = 0.999
    eps = 1e-8
    mu = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    nu = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    t = 0
    base_lr = 0.12

    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + max_seconds
    last_improve = start

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        elapsed = max(1e-9, time.monotonic() - start)
        progress = min(1.0, elapsed / max_seconds)

        stuck_limit = min(90.0, 0.30 * max_seconds)
        if time.monotonic() - last_improve > stuck_limit:
            if rng.random() < 0.35:
                inits = _param_inits(n, rng)
                if inits:
                    a = inits[int(rng.integers(0, len(inits)))]
                else:
                    a = _normalize_sum(best, 1.0)
            else:
                a = _normalize_sum(best, 1.0)
                noise = 0.12 + 0.22 * float(rng.random())
                a = a * (1.0 + noise * rng.normal(size=n))
                a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
                a = _normalize_sum(a, 1.0)

            z = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
            z = z - float(z.mean())
            z = np.clip(z, -30.0, 30.0)
            mu *= 0.35
            nu *= 0.35
            t = 0
            last_improve = time.monotonic()
            continue

        p = _softmax(z)
        b = _convolve_self(p, m_score)
        bpmax = float(b.max())
        cur_sc = float(2.0 * n * bpmax) if bpmax > 0.0 else float("inf")

        L = b.size
        active_frac = 0.0035 * (1.0 - 0.75 * progress) + 0.0004
        K = int(max(1, min(L, int(active_frac * float(L)))))
        if K <= 0 or K > L:
            K = L

        if rng.random() < 0.38:
            tau = bpmax * (0.010 + 0.070 * (1.0 - progress))
            tau = max(1e-12 * max(1.0, bpmax), tau)
            x = (b - bpmax) / tau
            x = np.clip(x, -60.0, 60.0)
            x = x - float(x.max())
            w = np.exp(x)
            ws = float(w.sum())
            if not np.isfinite(ws) or ws <= 0.0:
                w = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
            else:
                w = w / ws
            if w.sum() <= 0.0:
                w = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
            if K > 0:
                idx = np.argpartition(b, -K)[-K:]
                w[idx] += 0.40
                ws = float(w.sum())
                if ws <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(ws):
                    w = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
                    KE = max(1, K)
                    idx = np.argpartition(b, -KE)[-KE:]
                    w[idx] = 1.0 / float(KE)
                else:
                    w = w / ws
        else:
            w = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
            KE = max(1, min(K, L))
            idx = np.argpartition(b, -KE)[-KE:]
            w[idx] = 1.0 / float(KE)

        c = _grad_cross_from_weights(p, w, m_grad)
        gp = 2.0 * c
        avg = float(np.dot(p, gp))
        gz = p * (gp - avg)
        gz = gz - float(gz.mean())

        grms = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(gz * gz)))
        if not np.isfinite(grms) or grms < 1e-15:
            base_lr = max(0.02, 0.95 * base_lr)
            z = z + 0.05 * rng.normal(size=n)
            z = z - float(z.mean())
            z = np.clip(z, -30.0, 30.0)
            continue

        t += 1
        mu = beta1 * mu + (1.0 - beta1) * gz
        nu = beta2 * nu + (1.0 - beta2) * (gz * gz)
        b1hat = 1.0 - beta1 ** t
        b2hat = 1.0 - beta2 ** t
        mhat = mu / max(1e-12, b1hat)
        vhat = nu / max(1e-12, b2hat)
        step = mhat / (np.sqrt(vhat) + eps)
        step = np.clip(step, -2.0, 2.0)
        stprms = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(step * step)))
        if not np.isfinite(stprms) or stprms <= 0.0:
            continue
        step = step / stprms

        lr = base_lr * (1.0 - 0.65 * progress)
        best_zloc = z.copy()
        best_loc = cur_sc

        for factor in (1.0, 0.5):
            zn = z - lr * factor * step
            zn = zn - float(zn.mean())
            zn = np.clip(zn, -30.0, 30.0)
            pn = _softmax(zn)
            bn = _convolve_self(pn, m_score)
            sc2 = float(2.0 * n * bn.max()) if bn.size else float("inf")
            if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_loc:
                best_loc = sc2
                best_zloc = zn

        z = best_zloc

        if np.isfinite(best_loc) and best_loc < best_sc - 1e-12:
            best_sc = best_loc
            best_z = z.copy()
            best = _softmax(z)
            last_improve = time.monotonic()
        else:
            # Shrink learning rate a little if no local improvement.
            base_lr = max(0.02, 0.965 * base_lr)

    p = _softmax(best_z)
    sc = _score(p)
    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
        best_sc = sc
        best = p.copy()
    return _normalize_sum(best, 1.0), best_sc


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    seed = int(seed) if seed is not None else 42
    budget = float(max(60.0, float(budget_s)))
    global_start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = global_start + max(60.0, budget - 25.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    best_a = None
    best_sc = float("inf")
    candidates = []

    # Load previous sequence if available.
    prev = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    arr = _as_nonneg_array(prev)
    if arr is not None and arr.size >= 4 and float(arr.sum()) > 0.0:
        a_prev = _normalize_sum(arr, 1.0)
        sc_prev = _score(a_prev)
        if np.isfinite(sc_prev):
            best_a, best_sc = _register_candidate(best_a, best_sc, a_prev, sc_prev, candidates)
            print("Initial previous: n=%d score=%.8f" % (len(a_prev), best_sc))
        else:
            print("Previous sequence invalid by FFT score; ignoring.")
    else:
        print("No Previous sequence usable; using parametric starts.")
        for n0 in (512, 1024, 2048):
            inits = _param_inits(n0, rng)
            for a in inits:
                sc = _score(a)
                if np.isfinite(sc):
                    best_a, best_sc = _register_candidate(best_a, best_sc, a, sc, candidates)
            print("Parametric start after n=%d best=%.8f" % (n0, best_sc))
            if best_sc < 1.58:
                break

    if best_a is None:
        best_a = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) / 1024.0
        best_sc = 2.0
        candidates = [(best_sc, best_a.copy())]

    # Stage 1: LP polishing at length <= 2048.
    n_lp = int(best_a.size) if best_a.size <= 2048 else 2048
    if n_lp < 64:
        n_lp = 1024
    a_lp = _interp(best_a, n_lp)

    remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if remaining > 180.0 and linprog is not None:
        # Give more LP time when the current score is not yet close to target.
        if best_sc > 1.55:
            lp_frac = 0.42
        elif best_sc > 1.51:
            lp_frac = 0.34
        else:
            lp_frac = 0.18
        lp_time = min(960.0, max(120.0, remaining * lp_frac))
        lp_deadline = min(deadline - 90.0, time.monotonic() + lp_time)
        print("Stage LP n=%d budget=%.1fs current_best=%.8f" % (n_lp, lp_time, best_sc))
        a_lp2, sc_lp2 = _lp_stage(a_lp, lp_deadline, max_n=2048, rng=rng)
        best_a, best_sc = _register_candidate(best_a, best_sc, a_lp2, sc_lp2, candidates)
        print("After LP best=%.8f n=%s" % (best_sc, len(best_a) if best_a is not None else 0))
    else:
        print("Skipping LP stage (insufficient time or scipy unavailable).")

    # Stage 2: Adam polishing at the LP length.
    remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if remaining > 150.0:
        adam_time = min(520.0, max(60.0, 0.22 * remaining))
        adam_deadline = time.monotonic() + adam_time
        a_ad = _interp(best_a, n_lp)
        print("Stage Adam n=%d budget=%.1fs current_best=%.8f" % (n_lp, adam_time, best_sc))
        a_ad2, sc_ad2 = _optimize_adam(a_ad, n_lp, adam_deadline - time.monotonic(), int(rng.integers(0, 2**31 - 1)))
        best_a, best_sc = _register_candidate(best_a, best_sc, a_ad2, sc_ad2, candidates)
        print("After Adam n=%d best=%.8f" % (n_lp, best_sc))

    # Stage 3: try larger lengths.
    cur_n = int(best_a.size) if best_a is not None else 2048
    larger = []
    for L in (2048, 4096, 8192, 12288):
        if L > cur_n and L <= 16384:
            larger.append(L)

    # If already at 8192/12288, try one different same-size restart if time allows.
    for L in larger[:2]:
        remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if remaining < 220.0:
            break
        if L == len(larger):  # not possible, but keep simple
            alloc = max(120.0, remaining - 30.0)
        else:
            alloc = max(120.0, 0.46 * (remaining - 60.0))
        a0 = _interp(best_a, L)
        print("Stage larger Adam n=%d budget=%.1fs current_best=%.8f" % (L, alloc, best_sc))
        a1, sc1 = _optimize_adam(a0, L, alloc, int(rng.integers(0, 2**31 - 1)))
        best_a, best_sc = _register_candidate(best_a, best_sc, a1, sc1, candidates)
        print("After n=%d best=%.8f" % (L, best_sc))

    # Stage 4: final polish at current best length if time remains.
    remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if remaining > 100.0 and best_a is not None and best_a.size <= 12288:
        final_time = max(60.0, remaining - 20.0)
        print("Stage final polish n=%d budget=%.1fs current_best=%.8f" % (len(best_a), final_time, best_sc))
        a1, sc1 = _optimize_adam(best_a, int(best_a.size), final_time, int(rng.integers(0, 2**31 - 1)))
        best_a, best_sc = _register_candidate(best_a, best_sc, a1, sc1, candidates)
        print("Final polish best=%.8f" % best_sc)

    if best_a is None:
        best_a = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) / 1024.0
        best_sc = 2.0

    # Return normalized to sum 1 (evaluation is homogeneous).
    best_a = _normalize_sum(best_a, 1.0)
    best_a = np.clip(best_a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(best_a.sum())
    if s <= 0.01:
        best_a = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64)
        best_sc = 2.0

    # Optional exact validation for modest sizes.  The external evaluator will
    # in any case compute the official value.
    try:
        if best_a.size <= 6000 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 3.0:
            exact = evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in best_a])
            print("Final FFT score=%.8f exact score=%s" % (best_sc, exact))
            if not np.isfinite(exact):
                # Extremely defensive fallback.
                best_a = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64)
                best_sc = 2.0
    except Exception as e:
        print("Exact validation skipped: %s" % e)

    return [float(x) for x in best_a]
```
